# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-04)

## Corpus Check
- 5065 files · ~11,112,014 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 115 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 34, .css 18, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 68184 nodes · 195631 edges · 1395 communities (1260 shown, 135 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 8218 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `2d41675a`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
